Purpose\n\nDelete an account. exactly one argument. |\n| Success stdout | Two lines: `deleted`, then the code. |\n| Absent account | Exit 4, nothing on stdout. |\n| Account in use | Exit 3 when the account has any transaction; nothing is deleted and nothing is written to stdout. |\n| Check order | Existence and in-use checks complete before the delete. |\n| Wrong argument count | Exit 2, nothing on stdout. |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-005` — write: delete an account - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm the account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: find transactions that reference the account - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-delete-success cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code = \"ADELS1\"\n    db.accounts.create(account=code, name=\"Delete Me\")\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", code)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows == [[\"deleted\"], [code]]\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=code) is None\n=== END AC account-delete-success ===\n\n=== AC account-delete-in-use ===\nIntent: Deleting an account that has transactions is refused with exit 3 and changes nothing.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code = \"ADELU1\"\n    cat = \"ADELUC1\"\n    db.accounts.create(account=code, name=\"In Use\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"In Use Cat\")\n    txn_id = max([t.txn_id for t in db.transactions.list()], default=0) + 1\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=code,\n        category=cat, amount=500, memo=\"\",\n    )\n    accounts_before = len(db.accounts.list())\n    txns_before = len(db.transactions.list())\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", code)\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 3\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=code) is not None\n    assert Account Duplicate Rejection\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Rejects `ledger account add` for a code that already exists, leaving the accounts table unchanged. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-AccountAdd.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | duplicate account rejection |\n| Consumes | accounts.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn account code is a primary key. Adding an existing code is a rejected command: it exits 3, writes\nnothing to stdout, and neither the existing row nor any other row changes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n| Aspect | Rule |\n|--------|------|\n| Detection | The duplicate is detected by checking for the code before the first write, not by catching a late storage error. |\n| Result | Exit 3, nothing on stdout, one diagnostic line on stderr. |\n| State | The existing account keeps its original name; the account count is unchanged. |\n| Recovery | A later add of a different code succeeds. |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: check whether the code already exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-duplicate-rejected ===\nIntent: code = \"ADUPR1\"\n    original = \"Original Name\"\n    db.accounts.create(account=code, name=original)\n    before = len(db.accounts.list())\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", code, \"Different Name\")\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 3\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert len(db.accounts.list()) == before\n    got = db.accounts.get(account=code)\n    assert got is not None\n    assert got.name == original\n=== END AC account-duplicate-rejected ===\n\n=== AC account-duplicate-same-name ===\nIntent: Re-adding an existing account with the identical name is still |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger txn post`, recording one receipt or payment against an account in integer cents. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-AccountAdd.md, FEATURE-LoadCategories.md, FEATURE-LedgerCore.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger txn post |\n| Consumes | transactions.create, transactions.list, accounts.list, categories.get |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nPost a single transaction. Every input and the non-negative balance rule are checked before the first\nwrite, so a rejected post changes nothing.\n\n## Command Contract\n\n| Aspect | Rule |\n|--------|------|\n| Invocation | `ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]`: four or five arguments. |\n| Amount | Matches `-?DIGITS[.D[D]]`, at most two decimals, never zero. Parsed to integer cents by the shared core; no floating point. |\n| Date | A real calendar date written `YYYY-MM-DD`. |\n| Memo | Optional, 0 to 60 characters, no comma or newline. Absent means the empty string. |\n| Account and category | Both must exist; otherwise exit 3. |\n| Transaction id | One more than the largest `txn_id` present, or `1` in an empty table. |\n| Balance | If the account balance after the post would be below zero, exit 3 before any write. |\n| Success stdout | Header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`, then the new row with the amount as two-decimal text. |\n| Rejection | Exit 3, nothing on stdout, no row stored. |\n| Wrong argument count | Exit 2, nothing on stdout. |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — write: create a transaction - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: list transactions to find the largest id and the account balance - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm the account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-001` — read: confirm the category exists - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-success ===\nIntent: Posting a db_session() as db:\n    acct = \"TPOSS1\"\n    cat = \"TPOSSC1\"\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Post Success\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Post Success Cat\")\n    next_id = max([t.txn_id for t in db.transactions.list()], default=0) + 1\n    amount_text = \"45.50\"\n    day = \"2026-04-01\"\n    memo = \"walk in sale\"\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", acct, cat, amount_text, day, memo)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows[0] == [\"txn_id\", |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger account delete <code>`, removing an account that has no transactions. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-AccountGet.md, Purpose\n\nDelete an account. exactly one argument. |\n| Success stdout | Two lines: `deleted`, then the code. |\n| Absent account | Exit 4, nothing on stdout. |\n| Account in use | Exit 3 when the account has any transaction; nothing is deleted and nothing is written to stdout. |\n| Check order | Existence and in-use checks complete before the delete. |\n| Wrong argument count | Exit 2, nothing on stdout. |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-005` — write: delete an account - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm the account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: None\n=== END AC account-delete-success ===\n\n=== AC account-delete-in-use ===\nIntent: Deleting an account that has transactions is refused with exit 3 and changes nothing.\n\nfrom txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=code,\n        category=cat, amount=500, memo=\"\",\n    )\n    accounts_before = len(db.accounts.list())\n    txns_before = len(db.transactions.list())\n Account Duplicate Rejection\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Rejects `ledger account add` for a code that already exists, leaving the accounts table unchanged. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-AccountAdd.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | duplicate account rejection |\n| Consumes | accounts.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn account code is a primary key. Adding an existing code is a rejected command: it exits 3, writes\nnothing to stdout, and neither the existing row nor any other row changes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n| Aspect | Rule |\n|--------|------|\n| Detection | The duplicate is detected by checking for the code before the first write, not by catching a late storage error. |\n| Result | Exit 3, nothing on stdout, one diagnostic line on stderr. |\n| State | The existing account keeps its original name; the account count is unchanged. |\n| Recovery | A later add of a different code succeeds. |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: check whether the code already exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-duplicate-rejected ===\nIntent: Adding an existing account code exits 3, prints nothing, and keeps the original name.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code = \"ADUPR1\"\n    original = \"Original Name\"\n    db.accounts.create(account=code, name=original)\n    before = len(db.accounts.list())\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", code, \"Different Name\")\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 3\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert len(db.accounts.list()) == before\n    got = db.accounts.get(account=code)\n    assert got is not None\n    assert got.name == original\n=== END AC account-duplicate-rejected ===\n\n=== AC account-duplicate-same-name ===\nIntent: Re-adding an existing account with the identical name is still rejected with exit 3.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code = \"ADUPS1\"\n    name = \"Same Name\"\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", code, name)\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 0\n    before = len(db.accounts.list())\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", code, name)\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 3\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert len(db.accounts.list()) == before\n=== END AC account-duplicate-same-name ===\n\n=== AC account-duplicate-then-recover ===\nIntent: After a duplicate rejection, adding a different code succeeds and adds exactly one row.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code = \"ADUPV1\"\n    other = \"ADUPV2\"\n    db.accounts.create(account=code, name=\"First\")\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", code, \"Again\")\n    assert rc == 3\n    before = len(db.accounts.list())\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", other, \"Second\")\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 0\n    assert len(db.accounts.list()) == before + 1\n    got = db.accounts.get(account=other)\n    assert got is not None\n    assert got.name == \"Second\"\n=== END AC account-duplicate-then-recover ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A rejected duplicate changes no row.\n- Nothing is written to stdout on failure.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TxnPost.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Post\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger txn post`, recording one receipt or payment against an account in integer cents. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-AccountAdd.md, FEATURE-LoadCategories.md, FEATURE-LedgerCore.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger txn post |\n| Consumes | transactions.create, transactions.list, accounts.list, categories.get |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nPost a single transaction. Every input and the non-negative balance rule are checked before the first\nwrite, so a rejected post changes nothing.\n\n## Command Contract\n\n| Aspect | Rule |\n|--------|------|\n| Invocation | `ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]`: four or five arguments. |\n| Amount | Matches `-?DIGITS[.D[D]]`, at most two decimals, never zero. Parsed to integer cents by the shared core; no floating point. |\n| Date | A real calendar date written `YYYY-MM-DD`. |\n| Memo | Optional, 0 to 60 characters, no comma or newline. Absent means the empty string. |\n| Account and category | Both must exist; otherwise exit 3. |\n| Transaction id | One more than the largest `txn_id` present, or `1` in an empty table. |\n| Balance | If the account balance after the post would be below zero, exit 3 before any write. |\n| Success stdout | Header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`, then the new row with the amount as two-decimal text. |\n| Rejection | Exit 3, nothing on stdout, no row stored. |\n| Wrong argument count | Exit 2, nothing on stdout. |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — write: create a transaction - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: list transactions to find the largest id and the account balance - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm the account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-001` — read: confirm the category exists - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-success ===\nIntent: Posting a transaction prints its row with the next id and stores integer cents that read back.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    acct = \"TPOSS1\"\n    cat = \"TPOSSC1\"\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Post Success\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Post Success Cat\")\n    next_id = max([t.txn_id for t in db.transactions.list()], default=0) + 1\n    amount_text = \"45.50\"\n    day = \"2026-04-01\"\n    memo = \"walk in sale\"\n    rc, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", acct, cat, amount_text, day, memo)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert rc == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows[0] == [\"txn_id\", \"date\", \"account\", \"category\", \"amount\", \"memo\"]\n    assert len(rows) == 2\n    assert int(rows[1][0]) == next_id\n    assert rows[1][1] == day\n    assert rows[1][2] == acct\n    assert rows[1][3] == cat\n    assert rows[1][4] == amount_text\n    assert rows[1][5] == memo\n    got = db.transactions.get(txn_id=next_id)\n    assert got is not None\n    assert got.date == day\n    assert got.account == acct\n    assert got.category == cat\n    assert got.amount == int(amount_text.replace(\".\", \"\"))\n    assert got.memo == memo\n=== END AC txn-post-success ===\n\n=== AC
